{\n  outputError,\n  outputText,\n  outputWarning,\n} from '../../presentation/output/default-output.ts';\n/**\n * @module infra/database/operation-runner\n */\n\nimport { join } from '@std/path';\n\nimport { SCAFFOLD_DIRS } from '../../constants/scaffold/scaffold-dirs.ts';\nimport { SCAFFOLD_FILES } from '../../constants/scaffold/scaffold-files.ts';\nimport type { DbOperationRequest, DiscoveredDatabase } from '../../domain/db-engine.ts';\nimport {\n  type AppHostLifecycleLease,\n  type AppHostLifecycleLock,\n  FileAppHostLifecycleLock,\n} from './apphost-lifecycle-lock.ts';\nimport {\n  type AspireCommandExecutor,\n  type AspireCommandOptions,\n  type CommandOutput,\n  DenoAspireCommandExecutor,\n} from './aspire-command-executor.ts';\nimport {\n  buildAspireArgs,\n  buildDbCliEnv,\n  buildExecutableDisplayName,\n  findExecutableStatus,\n  findRunningAppHost,\n  resolveDbCliTimeoutSeconds,\n  TERMINAL_RESOURCE_STATES,\n} from './operation-runner-helpers.ts';\n\nconst DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 1_000;\nconst WAIT_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODES: ReadonlySet<number> = new Set([17, 18]);\n\ninterface DbOperationRunnerOptions {\n  readonly executor?: AspireCommandExecutor;\n  readonly lifecycleLock?: AppHostLifecycleLock;\n  readonly pollIntervalMs?: number;\n  readonly timeoutMs?: number;\n  readonly sleep?: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;\n  readonly writeOperationRequest?: (path: string, env: Record<string, string>) => Promise<void>;\n  readonly removeOperationRequest?: (path: string) => Promise<void>;\n  readonly registerSignalAbort?: (abort: () => void) => () => void;\n}\n\n/** Executes database operations through a resident AppHost or a scoped standalone host. */\nexport class DbOperationRunner {\n  private readonly executor: AspireCommandExecutor;\n  private readonly lifecycleLock: AppHostLifecycleLock;\n  private readonly pollIntervalMs: number;\n  private readonly timeoutMs: number;\n  private readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;\n  private readonly writeOperationRequest: (\n    path: string,\n    env: Record<string, string>,\n  ) => Promise<void>;\n  private readonly removeOperationRequest: (path: string) => Promise<void>;\n  private readonly registerSignalAbort: (abort: () => void) => () => void;\n\n  constructor(options: DbOperationRunnerOptions = {}) {\n    this.executor = options.executor ?? new DenoAspireCommandExecutor();\n    this.lifecycleLock = options.lifecycleLock ?? new FileAppHostLifecycleLock();\n    this.pollIntervalMs = options.pollIntervalMs ?? DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL_MS;\n    this.timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? resolveDbCliTimeoutSeconds() * 1_000;\n    this.sleep = options.sleep ??\n      ((ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)));\n    this.writeOperationRequest = options.writeOperationRequest ??\n      ((path, env) => Deno.writeTextFile(path, JSON.stringify(env)));\n    this.removeOperationRequest = options.removeOperationRequest ?? (async (path) => {\n      try {\n        await Deno.remove(path);\n      } catch (error) {\n        if (!(error instanceof Deno.errors.NotFound)) throw error;\n      }\n    });\n    this.registerSignalAbort = options.registerSignalAbort ?? registerSignalAbort;\n  }\n\n  /** Execute a database operation and return its process exit code. */\n  async execute(request: DbOperationRequest): Promise<number> {\n    const databases = request.target.kind === 'all'\n      ? request.target.databases\n      : [request.target.database];\n\n    if (request.operation === 'studio') {\n      return await this.executeOne(request, databases[0]);\n    }\n\n    const controller = new AbortController();\n    const unregisterSignals = this.registerSignalAbort(() => controller.abort());\n    try {\n      for (const database of databases) {\n        const code = await this.executeOne(request, database, controller.signal);\n        if (code !== 0) return code;\n      }\n    } finally {\n      unregisterSignals();\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  private async executeOne(\n    request: DbOperationRequest,\n    database: DiscoveredDatabase,\n    signal?: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    const aspireDir = join(request.projectRoot, SCAFFOLD_DIRS.ASPIRE_TS);\n    const apphostPath = join(aspireDir, SCAFFOLD_FILES.APPHOST_MTS);\n    const env = buildDbCliEnv(\n      request.operation,\n      database.configKey,\n      request.migrationName,\n      request.interactive,\n    );\n\n    if (request.operation === 'studio') {\n      return await this.executeInteractive(apphostPath, aspireDir, env);\n    }\n    if (!signal) throw new Error('Database operation cancellation signal is required.');\n\n    const lease = await this.lifecycleLock.acquire(apphostPath, {\n      timeoutMs: this.timeoutMs,\n      pollIntervalMs: this.pollIntervalMs,\n      sleep: this.sleep,\n    });\n    try {\n      const runningAppHost = await this.findProjectAppHost(apphostPath, aspireDir, signal);\n      if (runningAppHost) {\n        return await this.executeOnAppHost(\n          request,\n          database.configKey,\n          apphostPath,\n          aspireDir,\n          env,\n          signal,\n        );\n      }\n      return await this.executeStandalone(\n        request,\n        database.configKey,\n        apphostPath,\n        aspireDir,\n        env,\n        signal,\n      );\n    } finally {\n      await this.releaseLease(lease, apphostPath);\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async executeInteractive(\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    env: Record<string, string>,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    return await this.executor.spawn(buildAspireArgs('run', apphostPath), {\n      cwd: aspireDir,\n      env,\n      stdin: 'inherit',\n      stdout: 'inherit',\n      stderr: 'inherit',\n    });\n  }\n\n  private async executeStandalone(\n    request: DbOperationRequest,\n    configKey: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    env: Record<string, string>,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    outputText(`Starting the project AppHost for db ${request.operation}...`);\n    await this.runAspire(\n      buildAspireArgs('start', apphostPath, { isolated: true }),\n      { cwd: aspireDir, env, signal },\n    );\n\n    try {\n      return await this.executeOnAppHost(\n        request,\n        configKey,\n        apphostPath,\n        aspireDir,\n        env,\n        signal,\n      );\n    } finally {\n      await this.stopStandaloneAppHost(apphostPath, aspireDir);\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async executeOnAppHost(\n    request: DbOperationRequest,\n    configKey: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    env: Record<string, string>,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    outputText(`Starting db ${request.operation} for ${configKey}...`);\n    await this.waitForDatabase(configKey, apphostPath, aspireDir, signal);\n\n    if (isTypedResourceOperation(request.operation) && !request.migrationName) {\n      return await this.executeTypedResourceCommand(\n        request.operation,\n        configKey,\n        apphostPath,\n        aspireDir,\n        signal,\n      );\n    }\n    return await this.executeLegacyResource(\n      request.operation,\n      configKey,\n      apphostPath,\n      aspireDir,\n      env,\n      signal,\n    );\n  }\n\n  private async findProjectAppHost(\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<boolean> {\n    const output = await this.runAspire(\n      ['ps', '--format', 'Json', '--nologo', '--non-interactive'],\n      { cwd: aspireDir, signal },\n    );\n    return findRunningAppHost(output.stdout, apphostPath);\n  }\n\n  private async waitForDatabase(\n    configKey: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const timeoutSeconds = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.timeoutMs / 1_000));\n    const output = await this.executor.output(\n      [\n        'wait',\n        configKey,\n        '--status',\n        'healthy',\n        '--timeout',\n        String(timeoutSeconds),\n        '--apphost',\n        apphostPath,\n        '--non-interactive',\n        '--nologo',\n      ],\n      { cwd: aspireDir, signal },\n    );\n    if (output.code === 0) return;\n\n    const details = commandDetails(output);\n    if (WAIT_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODES.has(output.code)) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Database resource ${configKey} did not become healthy within ${timeoutSeconds} seconds ` +\n          `(aspire wait exit ${output.code}). Check its resource logs and listener readiness. ` +\n          `Aspire reported: ${details}`,\n      );\n    }\n    throw new Error(`aspire wait failed with exit code ${output.code}: ${details}`);\n  }\n\n  private async executeTypedResourceCommand(\n    operation: 'migrate' | 'seed' | 'reset',\n    configKey: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    const timeoutSeconds = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.timeoutMs / 1_000));\n    const args = [\n      'resource',\n      `${configKey}-cli`,\n      operation,\n      '--timeout',\n      String(timeoutSeconds),\n    ];\n    if (operation === 'reset') args.push('--confirm', 'true');\n    args.push('--apphost', apphostPath, '--non-interactive', '--nologo');\n\n    const output = await this.executor.output(args, { cwd: aspireDir, signal });\n    if (output.stdout.trim()) outputText(output.stdout.trim());\n    if (output.stderr.trim()) outputError(output.stderr.trim());\n    if (output.code === 0) outputText(`db ${operation} completed successfully.`);\n    return output.code;\n  }\n\n  private async executeLegacyResource(\n    operation: DbOperationRequest['operation'],\n    configKey: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    env: Record<string, string>,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    const resourceName = `${configKey}-cli`;\n    const operationRequestPath = join(aspireDir, `.netscript-db-operation-${configKey}.json`);\n    await this.writeOperationRequest(operationRequestPath, env);\n    try {\n      await this.runAspire(\n        [\n          'resource',\n          resourceName,\n          'start',\n          '--apphost',\n          apphostPath,\n          '--non-interactive',\n          '--nologo',\n        ],\n        { cwd: aspireDir, signal },\n      );\n      const code = await this.waitForExecutableCompletion(\n        operation,\n        configKey,\n        apphostPath,\n        aspireDir,\n        signal,\n      );\n      await this.printResourceLogs(resourceName, apphostPath, aspireDir, signal);\n      if (code === 0) {\n        outputText(`db ${operation} completed successfully.`);\n      } else {\n        outputError(`db ${operation} failed with exit code ${code}.`);\n      }\n      return code;\n    } finally {\n      await this.stopResource(resourceName, apphostPath, aspireDir);\n      await this.removeOperationRequest(operationRequestPath);\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async waitForExecutableCompletion(\n    operation: DbOperationRequest['operation'],\n    configKey: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<number> {\n    const displayName = buildExecutableDisplayName(operation, configKey);\n    const deadline = Date.now() + this.timeoutMs;\n\n    while (Date.now() <= deadline) {\n      const output = await this.runAspire(\n        [\n          'describe',\n          '--apphost',\n          apphostPath,\n          '--format',\n          'Json',\n          '--non-interactive',\n          '--nologo',\n        ],\n        { cwd: aspireDir, signal },\n      );\n      const resource = findExecutableStatus(output.stdout, apphostPath, displayName);\n      if (resource && resource.state && TERMINAL_RESOURCE_STATES.has(resource.state)) {\n        if (typeof resource.exitCode === 'number') return resource.exitCode;\n        throw new Error(\n          `Aspire resource ${displayName} reached ${resource.state} without an exit code.`,\n        );\n      }\n      await this.sleep(this.pollIntervalMs);\n    }\n    throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for Aspire resource ${displayName} to complete.`);\n  }\n\n  private async printResourceLogs(\n    resourceName: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n    signal: AbortSignal,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const output = await this.executor.output(\n        [\n          'logs',\n          resourceName,\n          '--apphost',\n          apphostPath,\n          '--non-interactive',\n          '--nologo',\n        ],\n        { cwd: aspireDir, signal },\n      );\n      if (output.stdout.trim()) outputText(output.stdout.trim());\n      if (output.stderr.trim()) outputError(output.stderr.trim());\n    } catch {\n      // Log fetching is best-effort.\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async stopResource(\n    resourceName: string,\n    apphostPath: string,\n    aspireDir: string,\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const output = await this.executor.output(\n      [\n        'resource',\n        resourceName,\n        'stop',\n        '--apphost',\n        apphostPath,\n        '--non-interactive',\n        '--nologo',\n      ],\n      { cwd: aspireDir },\n    );\n    if (output.code !== 0) {\n      outputWarning(\n        `Failed to stop database operation resource ${resourceName}: ${commandDetails(output)}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async stopStandaloneAppHost(apphostPath: string, aspireDir: string): Promise<void> {\n    const output = await this.executor.output(\n      ['stop', '--apphost', apphostPath, '--non-interactive', '--nologo'],\n      { cwd: aspireDir },\n    );\n    if (output.code !== 0) {\n      outputWarning(\n        `Failed to stop standalone Aspire AppHost ${apphostPath}: ${commandDetails(output)}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async runAspire(\n    args: readonly string[],\n    options: AspireCommandOptions,\n  ): Promise<CommandOutput> {\n    const output = await this.executor.output(args, options);\n    if (output.code !== 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `aspire ${args[0]} failed with exit code ${output.code}: ${commandDetails(output)}`,\n      );\n    }\n    return output;\n  }\n\n  private async releaseLease(lease: AppHostLifecycleLease, apphostPath: string): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await lease.release();\n    } catch (error) {\n      outputWarning(\n        `Failed to release database AppHost lifecycle lock for ${apphostPath}: ${\n          error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)\n        }`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction isTypedResourceOperation(\n  operation: DbOperationRequest['operation'],\n): operation is 'migrate' | 'seed' | 'reset' {\n  return operation === 'migrate' || operation === 'seed' || operation === 'reset';\n}\n\nfunction commandDetails(output: CommandOutput): string {\n  return output.stderr.trim() || output.stdout.trim() || 'unknown Aspire error';\n}\n\nfunction registerSignalAbort(abort: () => void): () => void {\n  const signals: Deno.Signal[] = Deno.build.os === 'windows' ? ['SIGINT'] : ['SIGINT', 'SIGTERM'];\n  for (const signal of signals) Deno.addSignalListener(signal, abort);\n  return () => {\n    for (const signal of signals) Deno.removeSignalListener(signal, abort);\n  };\n}\n","numLines":483,"startLine":1,"totalLines":483}}}

function generateDbCliMode(options: DbCliModeOptions): string {\n  const dbEntries = Object.entries(options.databases);\n  const targetCases = dbEntries.map(([name, entry]) => {\n    const engineTaskSuffix(engine: string): string {\n  switch (engine) {\n    case 'Postgres':\n      return 'postgres';\n    case 'Mysql':\n      return 'mysql';\n    case 'Mssql':\n      return 'mssql';\n    case 'Sqlite':\n      return 'sqlite';\n    default:\n      return engine.toLowerCase();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction engineDir(engine: string): string {\n  return engineTaskSuffix(engine);\n}\n\nfunction 13.5 `healthReports` contract. */\n63\texport function listenerReadinessWaitCommand(\n64\t  context: RunContext,\n65\t  expectation: ListenerReadinessExpectation,\n66\t): readonly string[] {\n67\t  return [\n68\t    'deno',\n69\t    'run',\n70\t    '--allow-run=aspire',\n71\t    `${context.project.repoRoot}/packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/runtime/verify-listener-readiness.ts`,\n72\t    context.project.appHost,\n73\t    expectation.resource,\n74\t    expectation.healthCheckKey,\n75\t    String(expectation.timeoutSeconds),\n76\t  ];\n77\t}\n78\t\n79\t/** Register the Phase-B failure/recovery fixture without executing it in Phase A. */\n80\texport function createListenerReadinessGates(\n81\t  database: DatabaseEngine = DATABASE.POSTGRES,\n82\t): readonly GateDefinition[] {\n83\t  return [\n84\t    commandGate(\n85\t      GATE.RUNTIME_HEALTH_LISTENER_UNREACHABLE,\n86\t      'Backing-service listeners fail unhealthy and recover',\n87\t      GATE_PHASE.RUNTIME,\n88\t      (context) => [\n89\t        'deno',\n90\t        'run',\n91\t        '--allow-read',\n92\t        '--allow-write',\n93\t        '--allow-run=aspire',\n94\t        `${context.project.repoRoot}/packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/runtime/listener-unreachable-fixture.ts`,\n95\t        context.project.appHost,\n96\t        context.project.projectRoot,\n97\t        database,\n98\t      ],\n99\t      (context) => context.project.projectRoot,\n100\t    ),\n101\t  ];\n102\t}\n103\t\n104\t/** Return the only two health checks the fault fixture is allowed to manipulate. */\n105\texport function listenerFaultExpectations(\n106\t  database: DatabaseEngine,\n107\t): readonly ListenerFaultExpectation[] {\n108\t  const garnet: ListenerFaultExpectation = {\n109\t    resource: ASPIRE_RESOURCE.GARNET,\n110\t    healthCheckKey: TEST_ONLY_GARNET_HEALTH_KEY,\n111\t    realHealthCheckKey: 'garnet_resp',\n112\t    controllerListener: 'garnet',\n113\t    timeoutSeconds: DEFAULT_LISTENER_WAIT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n114\t  };\n115\t  switch (database) {\n116\t    case DATABASE.POSTGRES:\n117\t      return [{\n118\t        resource: ASPIRE_RESOURCE.POSTGRES,\n119\t        healthCheckKey: TEST_ONLY_POSTGRES_HEALTH_KEY,\n120\t        realHealthCheckKey: 'postgres_listener',\n121\t        controllerListener: 'postgres',\n122\t        timeoutSeconds: DEFAULT_LISTENER_WAIT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n123\t      }, garnet];\n124\t    case DATABASE.MYSQL:\n125\t    case DATABASE.MSSQL:\n126\t    case DATABASE.SQLITE:\n127\t      return [garnet];\n128\t  }\n129\t}\n130\t\n131\t/** Parse the closed database axis used by D-101 subprocess arguments. */\n132\texport function parseListenerFaultDatabase(value: string): DatabaseEngine {\n133\t  switch (value) {\n134\t    case DATABASE.POSTGRES:\n135\t    case DATABASE.MYSQL:\n136\t    case DATABASE.MSSQL:\n137\t    case DATABASE.SQLITE:\n138\t      return value;\n139\t    default:\n140\t      throw new Error(`unsupported listener-fault database: ${value}`);\n141\t  }\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\t/** Build the lease-backed S8 receipt gate for the PostgreSQL runtime suite. */\n145\texport function createTypedDbPhaseBGate(): GateDefinition {\n146\t  return commandGate(\n147\t    GATE.RUNTIME_TYPED_DB_PHASE_B,\n148\t Build the wait command that also verifies the 13.5 `healthReports` contract. */\nexport function listenerReadinessWaitCommand(\n  context: RunContext,\n  expectation: ListenerReadinessExpectation,\n): readonly string[] {\n  return [\n    'deno',\n    'run',\n    '--allow-run=aspire',\n    `${context.project.repoRoot}/packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/runtime/verify-listener-readiness.ts`,\n    context.project.appHost,\n    expectation.resource,\n    expectation.healthCheckKey,\n    String(expectation.timeoutSeconds),\n  ];\n}\n\n/** Register the Phase-B failure/recovery fixture without executing it in Phase A. */\nexport function createListenerReadinessGates(\n  database: DatabaseEngine = DATABASE.POSTGRES,\n): readonly GateDefinition[] {\n  return [\n    commandGate(\n      GATE.RUNTIME_HEALTH_LISTENER_UNREACHABLE,\n      'Backing-service listeners fail unhealthy and recover',\n      GATE_PHASE.RUNTIME,\n      (context) => [\n        'deno',\n        'run',\n        '--allow-read',\n        '--allow-write',\n        '--allow-run=aspire',\n        `${context.project.repoRoot}/packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/runtime/listener-unreachable-fixture.ts`,\n        context.project.appHost,\n        context.project.projectRoot,\n        database,\n      ],\n      (context) => context.project.projectRoot,\n    ),\n  ];\n}\n\n/** Return the only two health checks the fault fixture is allowed to manipulate. */\nexport function listenerFaultExpectations(\n  database: DatabaseEngine,\n): readonly ListenerFaultExpectation[] {\n  const garnet: ListenerFaultExpectation = {\n    resource: ASPIRE_RESOURCE.GARNET,\n    healthCheckKey: TEST_ONLY_GARNET_HEALTH_KEY,\n    realHealthCheckKey: 'garnet_resp',\n    controllerListener: 'garnet',\n    timeoutSeconds: DEFAULT_LISTENER_WAIT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n  };\n  switch (database) {\n    case DATABASE.POSTGRES:\n      return [{\n        resource: ASPIRE_RESOURCE.POSTGRES,\n        healthCheckKey: TEST_ONLY_POSTGRES_HEALTH_KEY,\n        realHealthCheckKey: 'postgres_listener',\n        controllerListener: 'postgres',\n        timeoutSeconds: DEFAULT_LISTENER_WAIT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n      }, garnet];\n    case DATABASE.MYSQL:\n    case DATABASE.MSSQL:\n    case DATABASE.SQLITE:\n      return [garnet];\n  }\n}\n\n/** Parse the closed database axis used by D-101 subprocess arguments. */\nexport function parseListenerFaultDatabase(value: string): DatabaseEngine {\n  switch (value) {\n    case DATABASE.POSTGRES:\n    case DATABASE.MYSQL:\n    case DATABASE.MSSQL:\n    case DATABASE.SQLITE:\n      return value;\n    default:\n      throw new Error(`unsupported listener-fault database: ${value}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Build the lease-backed S8 receipt gate for the PostgreSQL runtime suite. */\nexport function createTypedDbPhaseBGate(): GateDefinition {\n  return commandGate(\n    GATE.RUNTIME_TYPED_DB_PHASE_B,\n    'Verify typed database commands and bounded unhealthy wait',\n    GATE_PHASE.RUNTIME,\n    (context) => [\n      'deno',\n      'run',\n      '--allow-env=ASPIRE_CLI_START_TIMEOUT',\n      '--allow-read',\n      '--allow-write',\n      '--allow-run=aspire,deno',\n      `${context.project.repoRoot}/packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/runtime/verify-typed-db-phase-b.ts`,\n      context.project.appHost,\n      context.project.projectRoot,\n      context.project.cliEntrypoint.startsWith('jsr:')\n        ? context.project.cliEntrypoint\n        : resolve(context.project.repoRoot, context.project.cliEntrypoint),\n      context.request.options.database,\n    ],\n    (context) => context.project.projectRoot,\n  );\n}\n","numLines":109,"startLine":60,"totalLines":168}}}

readonly stderr: string;\n15\t}\n16\t\n17\tinterface RecordedCall {\n18\t  readonly args: readonly string[];\n19\t  readonly options: {\n20\t    readonly cwd: string;\n21\t    readonly env?: Record<string, string>;\n22\t    readonly signal?: AbortSignal;\n23\t  };\n24\t}\n25\t\n26\ttype OutputStep = CommandOutput | ((options: RecordedCall['options']) => Promise<CommandOutput>);\n27\t\n28\tclass FakeAspireExecutor {\n29\t  readonly outputCalls: RecordedCall[] = [];\n30\t  readonly spawnCalls: RecordedCall[] = [];\n31\t\n32\t  constructor(\n33\t    private readonly outputs: OutputStep[] = [],\n34\t    private readonly spawnCodes: number[] = [],\n35\t  ) {}\n36\t\n37\t  output(args: readonly string[], options: RecordedCall['options']): Promise<CommandOutput> {\n38\t    this.outputCalls.push({ args: [...args], options });\n39\t    const next = this.outputs.shift();\n40\t    if (!next) throw new Error(`Unexpected output() call: ${args.join(' ')}`);\n41\t    return typeof next === 'function' ? next(options) : Promise.resolve(next);\n42\t  }\n43\t\n44\t  spawn(args: readonly string[], options: RecordedCall['options']): Promise<number> {\n45\t    this.spawnCalls.push({ args: [...args], options });\n46\t    const next = this.spawnCodes.shift();\n47\t    if (next === undefined) throw new Error(`Unexpected spawn() call: ${args.join(' ')}`);\n48\t    return Promise.resolve(next);\n49\t  }\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\tclass FakeLock implements AppHostLifecycleLock {\n53\t  acquire(): Promise<AppHostLifecycleLease> {\n54\t    return Promise.resolve({ release: () => Promise.resolve() });\n55\t  }\n56\t}\n57\t\n58\tconst PROJECT_ROOT = 'C:\\\\repo\\\\sample-app';\n59\tconst APPHOST = join(PROJECT_ROOT, 'aspire', 'apphost.mts');\n60\tconst DATABASE: DiscoveredDatabase = {\n61\t  configKey: 'postgres',\n62\t  engine: 'postgres',\n63\t  databaseName: 'sample-app-db',\n64\t  workspaceDir: 'database/postgres',\n65\t  enabled: true,\n66\t};\n67\t\n68\tfunction request(\n69\t  operation: DbOperationRequest['operation'],\n70\t  migrationName?: string,\n71\t): DbOperationRequest {\n72\t  return {\n73\t    operation,\n74\t    target: { kind: 'single', database: DATABASE },\n75\t    projectRoot: PROJECT_ROOT,\n76\t    migrationName,\n77\t  };\n78\t}\n79\t\n80\tfunction output(code = 0, stdout = '', stderr = ''): CommandOutput {\n81\t  return { code, stdout, stderr };\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\tfunction running(path = APPHOST): CommandOutput {\n85\t  return output(0, JSON.stringify([{ appHostPath: path, appHostPid: 42 }]));\n86\t}\n87\t\n88\tfunction completedResource(exitCode = 0): CommandOutput {\n89\t  return output(\n90\t    0,\n91\t    JSON.stringify([{\n92\t      appHostPath: APPHOST,\n93\t      resources: [{\n94\t        displayName: 'postgres-cli',\n95\t        resourceType: 'Executable',\n96\t        state: 'Finished',\n97\t        exitCode,\n98\t      }],\n99\t    }]),\n100\t  );\n101\t}\n102\t\n103\tfunction runner(\n104\t  executor: FakeAspireExecutor,\n105\t  options: {\n106\t    readonly writeOperationRequest?: (path: string, env: Record<string, string>) => Promise<void>;\n107\t    readonly registerSignalAbort?: (abort: () => void) => () => void;\n108\t  } = {},\n109\t): DbOperationRunner {\n110\t  return new DbOperationRunner({\n111\t    executor,\n112\t    lifecycleLock: new FakeLock(),\n113\t    pollIntervalMs: 0,\n114\t    timeoutMs: 60_000,\n115\t    sleep: async () => {},\n116\t    writeOperationRequest: (() => Promise.resolve()),\n117\t    removeOperationRequest: () => Promise.resolve(),\n118\t    registerSignalAbort: options.registerSignalAbort ?? (() => () => {}),\n119\t  });\n120\t}\n121\t\n122\tdescribe('DbOperationRunner', () => {\n123\t  it('matches the project AppHost and routes migrate through its typed resource command', async () => @module infra/database/operation-runner_test */\n\nimport { join } from '@std/path';\nimport { assertEquals, assertRejects, assertStringIncludes } from 'jsr:@std/assert@^1';\nimport { describe, it } from 'jsr:@std/testing@^1/bdd';\n\nimport type { DbOperationRequest, DiscoveredDatabase } from '../../domain/db-engine.ts';\nimport type { AppHostLifecycleLease, AppHostLifecycleLock } from './apphost-lifecycle-lock.ts';\nimport { DbOperationRunner } from './operation-runner.ts';\n\ninterface CommandOutput {\n  readonly code: number;\n  readonly stdout: string;\n  readonly stderr: string;\n}\n\ninterface RecordedCall {\n  readonly args: readonly string[];\n  readonly options: {\n    readonly cwd: string;\n    readonly env?: Record<string, string>;\n    readonly signal?: AbortSignal;\n  };\n}\n\ntype OutputStep = CommandOutput | ((options: RecordedCall['options']) => Promise<CommandOutput>);\n\nclass FakeAspireExecutor {\n  readonly outputCalls: RecordedCall[] = [];\n  readonly spawnCalls: RecordedCall[] = [];\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly outputs: OutputStep[] = [],\n    private readonly spawnCodes: number[] = [],\n  ) {}\n\n  output(args: readonly string[], options: RecordedCall['options']): Promise<CommandOutput> {\n    this.outputCalls.push({ args: [...args], options });\n    const next = this.outputs.shift();\n    if (!next) throw new Error(`Unexpected output() call: ${args.join(' ')}`);\n    return typeof next === 'function' ? next(options) : Promise.resolve(next);\n  }\n\n  spawn(args: readonly string[], options: RecordedCall['options']): Promise<number> {\n    this.spawnCalls.push({ args: [...args], options });\n    const next = this.spawnCodes.shift();\n    if (next === undefined) throw new Error(`Unexpected spawn() call: ${args.join(' ')}`);\n    return Promise.resolve(next);\n  }\n}\n\nclass FakeLock implements AppHostLifecycleLock {\n  acquire(): Promise<AppHostLifecycleLease> {\n    return Promise.resolve({ release: () => Promise.resolve() });\n  }\n}\n\nconst PROJECT_ROOT = 'C:\\\\repo\\\\sample-app';\nconst APPHOST = join(PROJECT_ROOT, 'aspire', 'apphost.mts');\nconst DATABASE: DiscoveredDatabase = {\n  configKey: 'postgres',\n  engine: 'postgres',\n  databaseName: 'sample-app-db',\n  workspaceDir: 'database/postgres',\n  enabled: true,\n};\n\nfunction request(\n  operation: DbOperationRequest['operation'],\n  migrationName?: string,\n): DbOperationRequest {\n  return {\n    operation,\n    target: { kind: 'single', database: DATABASE },\n    projectRoot: PROJECT_ROOT,\n    migrationName,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction output(code = 0, stdout = '', stderr = ''): CommandOutput {\n  return { code, stdout, stderr };\n}\n\nfunction running(path = APPHOST): CommandOutput {\n  return output(0, JSON.stringify([{ appHostPath: path, appHostPid: 42 }]));\n}\n\nfunction completedResource(exitCode = 0): CommandOutput {\n  return output(\n    0,\n    JSON.stringify([{\n      appHostPath: APPHOST,\n      resources: [{\n        displayName: 'postgres-cli',\n        resourceType: 'Executable',\n        state: 'Finished',\n        exitCode,\n      }],\n    }]),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction runner(\n  executor: FakeAspireExecutor,\n  options: {\n    readonly writeOperationRequest?: (path: string, env: Record<string, string>) => Promise<void>;\n    readonly registerSignalAbort?: (abort: () => void) => () => void;\n  } = {},\n): DbOperationRunner {\n  return new DbOperationRunner({\n    executor,\n    lifecycleLock: new FakeLock(),\n    pollIntervalMs: 0,\n    timeoutMs: 60_000,\n    sleep: async () => {},\n    writeOperationRequest: options.writeOperationRequest ?? (() => Promise.resolve()),\n    removeOperationRequest: () => Promise.resolve(),\n    registerSignalAbort: options.registerSignalAbort ?? (() => () => {}),\n  });\n}\n\ndescribe('DbOperationRunner', () => {\n  it('matches the project AppHost and routes migrate through its typed resource command', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n      running(),\n      output(),\n      output(0, '{\"success\":true,\"message\":\"ok\"}'),\n    ]);\n\n    assertEquals(await runner(executor).execute(request('migrate')), 0);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.map((call) => call.args[0]), ['ps', 'wait', 'resource']);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls[0].args, [\n      'ps',\n      '--format',\n      'Json',\n      '--nologo',\n      '--non-interactive',\n    ]);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls[1].args, [\n      'wait',\n      'postgres',\n      '--status',\n      'healthy',\n      '--timeout',\n      '60',\n      '--apphost',\n      APPHOST,\n      '--non-interactive',\n      '--nologo',\n    ]);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls[2].args, [\n      'resource',\n      'postgres-cli',\n      'migrate',\n      '--timeout',\n      '60',\n      '--apphost',\n      APPHOST,\n      '--non-interactive',\n      '--nologo',\n    ]);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.some((call) => call.args[0] === 'start'), false);\n  });\n\n  it('starts and stops one scoped project AppHost when no matching AppHost is running', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n      output(0, '[]'),\n      output(0, '{\"appHostPid\":43}'),\n      output(),\n      output(0, '{\"success\":true,\"message\":\"ok\"}'),\n      output(),\n    ]);\n\n    assertEquals(await runner(executor).execute(request('seed')), 0);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.map((call) => call.args[0]), [\n      'ps',\n      'start',\n      'wait',\n      'resource',\n      'stop',\n    ]);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls[1].args, [\n      'start',\n      '--apphost',\n      APPHOST,\n      '--format',\n      'Json',\n      '--non-interactive',\n      '--isolated',\n      '--nologo',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('does not treat another project AppHost as the resident project', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n      running('C:\\\\repo\\\\another\\\\aspire\\\\apphost.mts'),\n      output(),\n      output(),\n      output(),\n      output(),\n    ]);\n\n    assertEquals(await runner(executor).execute(request('seed')), 0);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls[1].args[0], 'start');\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.at(-1)?.args[0], 'stop');\n  });\n\n  it('passes destructive confirmation to the typed reset command', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([running(), output(), output()]);\n\n    assertEquals(await runner(executor).execute(request('reset')), 0);\n    const resourceArgs = executor.outputCalls[2].args;\n    assertEquals(resourceArgs.slice(0, 3), ['resource', 'postgres-cli', 'reset']);\n    assertEquals(resourceArgs.slice(5, 7), ['--confirm', 'true']);\n  });\n\n  for (const exitCode of [17, 18]) {\n    it(`maps aspire wait exit ${exitCode} to an actionable bounded-readiness error`, async () => {\n      const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n        running(),\n        output(exitCode, '', 'resource remains unhealthy'),\n      ]);\n\n      const error = await assertRejects(\n        () => runner(executor).execute(request('init', 'init')),\n        Error,\n        `Database resource postgres did not become healthy within 60 seconds (aspire wait exit ${exitCode})`,\n      );\n      assertStringIncludes(error.message, 'Check its resource logs and listener readiness');\n      assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.map((call) => call.args[0]), ['ps', 'wait']);\n    });\n  }\n\n  it('reports non-timeout wait failures without misclassifying them', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n      running(),\n      output(9, '', 'authentication failed'),\n    ]);\n\n    await assertRejects(\n      () => runner(executor).execute(request('seed')),\n      Error,\n      'aspire wait failed with exit code 9: authentication failed',\n    );\n  });\n\n  it('keeps migration-name operations on the explicit-start compatibility resource', async () => {\n    let requestEnv: Record<string, string> | undefined;\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n      running(),\n      output(),\n      output(),\n      completedResource(),\n      output(0, 'migration complete'),\n      output(),\n    ]);\n\n    assertEquals(\n      await runner(executor, {\n        writeOperationRequest: (_path, env) => {\n          requestEnv = env;\n          return Promise.resolve();\n        },\n      }).execute(request('migrate', 'add-profile')),\n      0,\n    );\n    assertEquals(requestEnv?.PRISMA_MIGRATION_NAME, 'add-profile');\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.map((call) => call.args[0]), [\n      'ps',\n      'wait',\n      'resource',\n      'describe',\n      'logs',\n      'resource',\n    ]);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls[2].args.slice(0, 3), [\n      'resource',\n      'postgres-cli',\n      'start',\n    ]);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.at(-1)?.args.slice(0, 3), [\n      'resource',\n      'postgres-cli',\n      'stop',\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('returns a typed resource command failure without restarting the resident AppHost', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([\n      running(),\n      output(),\n      output(7, '', 'migration failed'),\n    ]);\n\n    assertEquals(await runner(executor).execute(request('migrate')), 7);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.some((call) => call.args[0] === 'start'), false);\n    assertEquals(executor.outputCalls.some((call) => call.args[0] === 'stop'), false);\n  });\n\n  it('keeps studio attached to the project AppHost', async () => {\n    const executor = new FakeAspireExecutor([], [0]);\n\n    assertEquals(await runner(executor).execute(request('studio')), 0);\n    assertEquals(executor.spawnCalls[0].args[0], 'run');\n    assertEquals(executor.spawnCalls[0].args.includes(APPHOST), true);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":308,"startLine":1,"totalLines":308}}}

  ]);\n34\t\n35\t    const output = await new Deno.Command(Deno.execPath(), {\n36\t      args: ['run', '--allow-all', runnerPath, errorPath, 'db:seed:postgres'],\n37\t      cwd: root,\n38\t { assertEquals, assertStringIncludes } from 'jsr:@std/assert@^1';\nimport { join } from 'jsr:@std/path@^1';\n\nconst ACTIONABLE_SEED_ERROR = 'Seed cause: required fixture users.json was not found.';\nconst ANSI_TASK_BANNER =\n  '\\u001b[0G\\u001b[2K\\u001b[JTask db:seed:postgres deno task db:seed\\u001b[0m';\n\nDeno.test('run-tool persists actionable seed stderr after an ANSI task banner', async () => {\n  const root = await Deno.makeTempDir({ prefix: 'netscript-run-tool-ansi-' });\n  const runnerPath = join(root, 'run-tool.mts');\n  const errorPath = join(root, 'seed.error');\n  try {\n    const template = await Deno.readTextFile(\n      new URL('../../../../assets/aspire/helpers/run-tool.ts.template', import.meta.url),\n    );\n    await Promise.all([\n --allow-all seed-failure.ts',\n          },\n        }),\n      ),\n      Deno.writeTextFile(\n        join(root, 'seed-failure.ts'),\n        `const detail = ${JSON.stringify(`${ANSI_TASK_BANNER}\\n${ACTIONABLE_SEED_ERROR}\\n`)};\nawait Deno.stderr.write(new TextEncoder().encode(detail));\nDeno.exit(16);\n`,\n      ),\n    ]);\n\n    const output = await new Deno.Command(Deno.execPath(), {\n      args: ['run', '--allow-all', runnerPath, errorPath, 'db:seed:postgres'],\n      cwd: root,\n      stdout: 'piped',\n      stderr: 'piped',\n    }).output();\n    const stderr = new TextDecoder().decode(output.stderr);\n\n    assertEquals(output.code, 16);\n    assertStringIncludes(stderr, ANSI_TASK_BANNER);\n    assertStringIncludes(stderr, ACTIONABLE_SEED_ERROR);\n    assertEquals((await Deno.readTextFile(errorPath)).trim(), ACTIONABLE_SEED_ERROR);\n  } finally {\n    await Deno.remove(root, { recursive: true });\n  }\n});\n","numLines":51,"startLine":1,"totalLines":51}}}

Hmm — the verify script uses `db migrate`, not `db init --name init`. Wait, the ruling says S8 owns GATE 1 ONLY on #863: \"the exact `netscript db init --name init` path plus resource/probe detail\". So `db init --name init` exercises the legacy explicit-start path with bounded wait. And the operation-runner test at line 217-231 covers exactly that: `request('init','init')` with wait exit 17/18 → bounded readiness error. But verify-typed-db-phase-b.ts tests `db migrate` bounded, not `db init --name init` bounded. Hmm — is that GATE 1 drift? The coordinator said \"S8 owns GATE 1 ONLY on #863 (the exact `netscript db init --name init` path plus resource/probe detail)\". The Phase-B verify script covers typed migrate bounded-wait, help surfaces, reset refusal — but the `db init --name init` path is covered in unit tests (operation-runner_test.ts:225) and NOT in the runtime verify script. Actually wait — `db init --name init` with migration name goes through legacy path in the runner. The Phase-B script's `db migrate` (no migration name) goes through the typed resource command path. Both paths are bounded via waitForDatabase. So runtime evidence covers typed path; init path only unit-tested. Is that a finding? Mildly — the ruling described GATE 1 as \"the exact `netscript db init --name init` path plus resource/probe detail\". The receipt covers `db migrate` rather than `db init --name init`. Since runtime is parked and receipts can't exist, and the unit test covers init bounded error mapping, I'd note it as a minor observation rather than a failure. Actually, let me check if `db init --name init` appears in verify scripts elsewhere or in behavior-scripts.ts. Let me grep.\n\nAlso check: `parseOperationRequest` in run-tool — operation regex `/^[a-z-]+$/` guards against injection into task name `db:${operation}:${taskSuffix}`. Good finite-vocab guard.\n\nOne more check: the `run-tool.ts.template` `--request` mode validation `if (!operation || !/^[a-z-]+$/.test(operation))` — but `request.NETSCRIPT_PRISMA_OPERATION` for 'init' is 'init'? buildDbCliEnv sets NETSCRIPT_PRISMA_OPERATION: operation where operation can be 'init'. Fine.\n\nPotential real defect candidates to weigh:\n\n1. `operation-runner.ts:96-99` — in `execute()`, when looping over multiple databases, if `code !== 0` it returns early — but the `finally { unregisterSignals() }` handles cleanup. Fine.\n\n2. `waitForDatabase` uses `this.executor.output` with `--timeout String(timeoutSeconds)` — fine.\n\n3. `executeTypedResourceCommand` — on failure returns output.code; stdout/stderr printed. But note: for the resident-AppHost path, the typed command runs through `aspire resource <name> migrate` — and this doesn't use `env`/operation request file. OK.\n\n4. In `run-tool.ts.template` request mode, `runTool` gets `env: request` — passing the full parsed JSON request as child env. This REPLACES the environment entirely? No — Deno.Command `env` option: if `env` is provided, it's merged with... Actually in Deno, `env` in CommandOptions *replaces* the environment? No — Deno's `env` option: \"Environment variables to set for the child process... by default the parent's env is inherited and `env` adds/overrides\"? Let me recall: In Deno, `Deno.Command` `env` — \"An object containing environment variables to set for the child. By default, environment variables from the parent process are inherited\" — actually Deno inherits parent env unless `clearEnv: true`. Yes, Deno merges. Fine.\n\n5. Timeout kill: SIGTERM only, no escalation to SIGKILL; if the child ignores SIGTERM, `child.status` hangs forever and `runTool` never returns — and the AppHost command handler would hang. Deno tasks (deno run) terminate on SIGTERM, so practical risk is low. Worth a minor note.\n\n6. `executeStandalone`: `runAspire(start ...)` then in `finally` `stopStandaloneAppHost`. If `runAspire(start)` itself throws (non-zero exit), the finally still tries `stop` — fine.\n\n7. `executeOne` typed path: after `waitForDatabase`, `executeTypedResourceCommand` — but the *typed command* internally re-resolves DATABASE_URL via `builder.getConfiguration().getConnectionString(target.resourceKey)` and spawns its own `deno run ... run-tool.mts errorFile db:migrate:postgres timeout`. Note the AppHost-side `executeDbTool` passes `timeoutSeconds` as the last arg — the run-tool non-request mode validates positive integer. Good symmetry.\n\n8. Notable asymmetry: `executeTypedResourceCommand` does NOT capture/pass the operation request file — but the AppHost command handler spawns the tool directly with env. Fine.\n\n9. `waitForExecutableCompletion` legacy: polls `describe` every pollIntervalMs until deadline; if resource not found, keeps polling until timeout. OK.\n\n10. `stopResource` in the legacy path runs even on failure — uses executor.output without signal, so cancellation (SIGINT) won't interrupt cleanup. OK-ish.\n\n11. `resource exclusion ownership`: `RESOURCE_DEFAULTS.DbCliModeExcludeFromMcp: true` in compat template; generated code reads `RESOURCE_DEFAULTS.DbCliModeExcludeFromMcp`. Gate: exclusion applied exactly once, after commands registered, per target. Test at 163-184. Good.\n\nNow check `generators-tools-db-index_test.ts` briefly? It asserts `await resource.excludeFromMcp();` at line 251. Fine.\n\nLet me check whether `db init --name init` appears anywhere in e2e runtime scripts (GATE 1 ruling mentions exact path). And confirm `DbOperationRequest.operation` vocabulary includes 'init'. Also check doctrine: finite vocabularies as constants — `WAIT_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODES` (17,18) is a named set. `TERMINAL_RESOURCE_STATES` named. Good.\n\nOne more check — the `runtime-gates_test.ts` first test asserts verify script content via string includes on the *file content*? It showed `command[2].includes('`${database}-cli`')` — reading verify-typed-db-phase-b.ts source text. That's shape-only on the gate script. A child that ignores SIGTERM hangs the AppHost command handler indefinitely — an unbounded path in a slice whose purpose is bounded waiting. Unlikely in practice (child is `deno task`), but the only graceful-path bound is the child's own cooperation. Required action: escalate to `SIGKILL` after a short grace, or race `child.status` against a hard deadline.\n2. **Low — GATE 1's ruled exact path (`netscript db init --name init`) is not a receipt entry.** `verify-typed-db-phase-b.ts:113-123` drives `db migrate --db <database>`; the `init`-with-migration-name request exercises the legacy explicit-start branch (`operation-runner.ts:121-123` → `executeLegacyResource`, since `isTypedResourceOperation` excludes `init`), and its bounded-wait evidence exists only at unit level (`operation-runner_test.ts:225-231`, `request('init','init')` with wait exits 17/18). The bounded mechanism itself (`waitForDatabase`, `operation-runner.ts:240-273`) is shared by both branches, so the mechanism is covered — but the exact ruled path has no runtime receipt field. Runtime is parked, so nothing can be captured now; when Phase-B unblocks, the receipt should include the `db init --name init` bounded-failure case, not only `db migrate`.\n3. **Low — failure-messaging asymmetry between typed and legacy paths.** `operation-runner.ts:293-297` prints the typed command's stderr but, unlike the legacy branch (`:335` `db ${operation} failed with exit code ${code}`), emits no explicit failure summary line on non-zero exit; the CLI caller only sees the process code plus streamed stderr. Cosmetic consistency drift, not a correctness defect; align if the CLI's exit reporting relies on that line.\n\n### Test adequacy\n\nMixed but net strong. `operation-runner_test.ts` is genuinely behavioural: exact argv for `ps`/`wait`/`resource` (`:131-161`), scoped-start/stop lifecycle (`:165-192`), foreign-AppHost rejection (`:194-206`), `--confirm true` propagation (`:208-215`), exit-code 17/18 → actionable bounded-readiness error text (`:217-231`), non-timeout non-misclassification (`:234-245`), typed failure returning 7 without restarting/stopping the resident AppHost (`:288-298`). `run-tool-template_test.ts` executes the emitted template in a temp dir and asserts real behaviour: ANSI banner stripped, actionable line persisted, exit code 16 propagated (`:43-46`). `generate-db-cli-mode_test.ts` is largely string-shape on emitted source — acceptable for a generator — but the reset-before-mutation ordering (`:123-133`) and exactly-once MCP exclusion (`:163-184`) constrain real control flow. Gap: the emitted `db-cli-mode.mts` is never compiled or executed in unit tests; its type-correctness rests on the parked `scaffold.runtime` E2E type-check. Doctrine holds: no `any`, no new lint-ignores, the one `as unknown as` in `_aspire-compat.ts.template:630` is pre-existing (no diff hit), IO stays at the executor/template edges, and vocabularies are finite constants (`DbOperation` union `db-engine.ts:113-125`, `WAIT_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODES` `operation-runner.ts:37`). The three findings are low severity and none blocks the slice; the SIGTERM escalation gap and the missing `db init --name init` receipt entry are the two worth scheduling when Phase-B runtime unblocks.
